\\\"搭建Orca项目\\\"\\n---\\n\\n# 龙虾Orca多Agent工作树脚手架 v1.0\\n\\n适用范围：所有在Orca IDE中使用多Agent协作开发的项目\\n生效范围：全域 / 永久 / Hermes + Codex + Claude Code + OpenCode + OpenClaw 统一\\n更新时间：2026-07-08\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 铁律（最高优先级）\\n\\n1. **幂等优先** — 所有初始化操作必须幂等，已初始化的分支绝不重复执行\\n2. **数据零丢失** — state.db 实时落盘，会话数据永不因重启丢失\\n3. **端口不硬编码** — Orca代理端口为临时分配，配置中使用模型后端直连URL\\n4. **标记文件守卫** — `.git/.orca_initialized.<branch>` 控制初始化生命周期\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 五步法流程\\n\\n### Step 1: 仓库初始化\\n```bash\\ncd <项目目录>\\ngit init\\ngit add .\\ngit commit -m \\\"init: 项目仓库初始化\\\"\\n```\\n\\n### Step 2: 创建分支与工作树\\n```bash\\ngit branch feature/<功能名>\\ngit worktree add worktrees/feature/<功能名> feature/<功能名>\\n```\\n\\n### Step 3: 配置幂等钩子\\n写入 `.git/hooks/post-checkout`（幂等版本），见附录A。\\n\\n### Step 4: 绑定Agent与模型后端\\n- 每个分支绑定一个Agent（Hermes/Codex/Claude Code）\\n- Codex配置 `OPENAI_BASE_URL` 指向实际模型后端（非Ollama占位）\\n\\n### Step 5: 验证与沉淀\\n```bash\\n# 验证标记文件\\nls .git/.orca_initialized.*\\n\\n# 验证worktree\\ngit worktree list\\n\\n# 验证会话持久化\\n# 在对话中输入：查看历史会话\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 附录A: 幂等post-checkout钩子\\n\\n```bash\\n#!/bin/bash\\nREPO_ROOT=\\\"$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel)\\\"\\nMARKER_FILE=\\\"$REPO_ROOT/.git/.orca_initialized\\\"\\n\\nif [ \\\"$3\\\" = \\\"1\\\" ]; then\\n    CURRENT_BRANCH=\\\"$(git rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD)\\\"\\n    BRANCH_MARKER=\\\"$MARKER_FILE.$(echo \\\"$CURRENT_BRANCH\\\" | tr '/' '_')\\\"\\n    if [ -f \\\"$BRANCH_MARKER\\\" ]; then\\n        exit 0\\n    fi\\n    echo \\\"[Orca] 初始化分支 $CURRENT_BRANCH...\\\"\\n    if [ -f \\\"$REPO_ROOT/orca_worktree_setup.sh\\\" ]; then\\n        bash \\\"$REPO_ROOT/orca_worktree_setup.sh\\\"\\n        date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S' > \\\"$BRANCH_MARKER\\\"\\n    fi\\nfi\\n```\\n\\n## 附录B: Codex配置模板\\n\\n```toml\\nmodel = \\\"mimo-v2.5-pro\\\"\\nOPENAI_API_KEY=\\\"<你的A..._URL = \\\"https://token-plan-cn.xiaomimimo.com/v1\\\"\\nmodel_provider = \\\"xiaomi-mimo\\\"\\n```\\n\\n## 附录C: 常见坑速查\\n\\n| 坑 | 根因 | 解法 |\\n|---|---|---|\\n| worktree分支checkout报错 | 分支已被worktree占用 | 在worktree目录直接操作 |\\n| Codex端口15721报错 | Orca临时端口重启后失效 | 更新OPENAI_BASE_URL到模型后端 |\\n| 会话历史面板空白 | workspaceSessionsByHostId重启清空 | 输入「查看历史会话」从state.db检索 |\\n| OPENAI_API_KEY无效 | 配置中为截断占位符 | 从.env读取真实key写入config.toml |\\n| 每次打开都重新初始化 | 钩子无幂等守卫 | 使用带标记文件的post-checkout |\\n\\n## 附录D: 跨Agent适配矩阵\\n\\n| Agent | 通信方式 | 配置文件 | 工作树绑定 |\\n|-------|---------|---------|-----------|\\n| Hermes | TUI (state.db) | config.yaml | 自动（Orca面板） |\\n| Codex | stdio (Orca代理) | .codex/config.toml | feature/python-script |\\n| Claude Code | CLI (stdio) | .claude/settings.json | review/audit-check |\\n| OpenCode | CLI | .opencode/config | 按需绑定 |\\n| OpenClaw | Gateway | openclaw.yaml | 按需绑定 |\\n\\n---\\n\\n完整详细文档见：`Doc/通用项目脚手架模板_v1.0.md`\\n\"}"}}], "anchor": true}, {"id": 2371, "role": "assistant", "content": "现在创建全域模板和龙虾五步法的迭代补充文档（保留原有内容不变，叠加新规则）。", "timestamp": 1783522429.9113538, "tool_calls": [{"id": "call_3a5f67bb30af43d28bfe8c30", "call_id": "call_3a5f67bb30af43d28bfe8c30", "response_item_id": "fc_3a5f67bb30af43d28bfe8c30", "type": "function", "function": {"name": "write_file", "arguments": "{\"content\": \"# 龙虾全域官方模板 v3.111 迭代补充\\n\\n> 版本：v3.111（叠加 Orca 多Agent工作树脚手架规范）\\n> 基于版本：v3.110（R51·全域AI Agent深度迭代）\\n> 更新时间：2026-07-08\\n> 变更类型：增量叠加（保留原有全部280项协议不变）\\n> 新增内容：Orca工作树管理规范、分支幂等机制、会话持久化规则\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 新增协议 #281: Orca 多Agent工作树管理规范\\n\\n### 适用范围\\n所有在 Orca IDE 中使用 Git worktree 进行多Agent协作开发的项目。\\n\\n### 规范内容\\n\\n#### 281.1 分支架构标准\\n- 主仓库 main 分支为稳定版本，禁止直接修改\\n- 每个功能分支通过 `git worktree add` 创建独立工作目录\\n- 分支命名：`feature/<功能名>`（开发）、`review/<审计名>`（审计）、`hotfix/<修复名>`（紧急修复）\\n- 每个分支绑定唯一Agent，职责不重叠\\n\\n#### 281.2 幂等初始化机制\\n- 所有分支初始化必须通过 `post-checkout` 钩子的幂等守卫控制\\n- 标记文件 `.git/.orca_initialized.<分支名>` 记录首次初始化时间\\n- 已标记的分支二次切换时自动跳过初始化\\n- 强制重新初始化：删除标记文件后切换分支\\n\\n#### 281.3 Orca HTTP/1.1 兼容\\n- Chromium 网络服务进程注入 `--disable-http2` 启动参数\\n- 解决 HTTP/2 协议错误导致的更新下载失败\\n- 验证方法：`wmic process where \\\"name='Orca.exe'\\\" get CommandLine | findstr disable-http2`\\n\\n#### 281.4 Agent 端口适配\\n- Orca 代理端口（如 15721）为临时分配，重启后失效\\n- Agent 配置中应使用模型后端直连 URL（如 MIMO），不依赖 Orca 代理\\n- Codex 配置：`OPENAI_BASE_URL = \\\"https://token-plan-cn.xiaomimimo.com/v1\\\"`\\n- 端口失效排查：`netstat -ano | grep LISTENING | grep <端口号>`\\n\\n#### 281.5 会话持久化\\n- Hermes state.db（SQLite）实时落盘，每条消息即时写入\\n- 会话数据不因 Orca 重启丢失\\n- Orca UI 面板的 `workspaceSessionsByHostId` 重启后清空属产品限制\\n- 检索方法：对话中输入「查看历史会话」或「查找XXX的历史对话」\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 新增协议 #282: 模型后端切换规范\\n\\n### 适用范围\\n所有 Agent 的模型后端配置与切换操作。\\n\\n### 规范内容\\n\\n#### 282.1 后端优先级\\n1. MIMO 代理（`token-plan-cn.xiaomimimo.com`）— 首选\\n2. Ollama 本地（`127.0.0.1:11434`）— 备选（需确保 Ollama 运行）\\n3. OpenAI 官方 — 需 API Key\\n\\n#### 282.2 切换流程\\n1. 测试新后端连通性：`curl <URL>/models -H \\\"Authorization: Bearer *** 更新 Agent 配置文件中的 `OPENAI_BASE_URL` 和 `OPENAI_API_KEY`\\n3. 重启 Agent 进程使配置生效\\n4. 发送测试指令验证\\n\\n#### 282.3 Key 管理\\n- API Key 存储在 `.env` 文件中，不硬编码在配置文件中\\n- 配置文件中的 Key 如被截断为占位符（如 `tp-cs3...puft`），需从 `.env` 重新写入\\n- 禁止在日志、报告、对话中输出完整 API Key\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 更新记录\\n\\n| 版本 | 日期 | 变更 |\\n|------|------|------|\\n| v3.110 | 2026-07-03 | R51深度迭代，280项协议 |\\n| v3.111 | 2026-07-08 | +2项协议（#281 Orca工作树管理、#282 模型后端切换） |\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 调用方式\\n\\n在任意对话中输入：\\n```\\n调用全域模板\\n```\\n或\\n```\\n按全域模板规范执行\\n```\\n\\n模板自动加载路径：`E:\\\\龙虾AI主控中心\\\\我的AI分身\\\\技能库\\\\龙虾全域官方模板-最终版.md`\\n本补充文档路径：`E:\\\\龙虾AI主控中心\\\\我的AI分身\\\\技能库\\\\龙虾全域模板_v3.111_迭代补充.md`\\n\", \"path\": \"E:\\\\龙虾AI主控中心\\\\我的AI分身\\\\技能库\\\\龙虾全域模板_v3.111_迭代补充.md\"}"}}, {"id": "call_3a6bfa6bbf7e41368185f60c", "call_id": "call_3a6bfa6bbf7e41368185f60c", "response_item_id": / 永久 / Codex + Hermes + Claude Code + OpenCode + OpenClaw + Marvis + WorkBuddy 统一\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 继承声明\\n\\n本版本**完整继承** V2.5 规则锁定版的全部内容，包括：\\n- 四大铁律（精准备份/无命令不修复/全存E盘/禁止后台扫描）\\n- 快照触发体系（手动确认/阈值自动/故障前置）\\n- 四大服务修复分支（Marvis/WorkBuddy/Codex/Claude Code）\\n- MT5量化MCP对接运维子流程\\n- MT5双环境隔离子流程\\n\\n**以下仅列出 V2.6 新增内容，原有规则不重复、不覆盖。**\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 新增：Orca 工作树运维子流程（V2.6）\\n\\n### 触发条件\\n当任务涉及以下场景时，自动激活本子流程：\\n- 在 Orca IDE 中创建/管理 Git worktree 分支\\n- 配置多Agent协作分支架构\\n- 排查 Agent 端口/连接问题\\n- 恢复丢失的会话历史\\n- 切换模型后端\\n\\n### Step 1: 目标定义\\n- 确认项目仓库路径、分支数量、Agent绑定方案\\n- 确认模型后端（MIMO/Ollama/OpenAI）及API Key来源\\n\\n### Step 2: 环境诊断\\n```bash\\n# 进程检查\\ntasklist | grep -iE \\\"orca|codex|hermes\\\"\\n# 端口检查\\nnetstat -ano | grep LISTENING\\n# 标记文件检查\\nls .git/.orca_initialized.*\\n# 数据库完整性\\nsqlite3 state.db \\\"PRAGMA integrity_check\\\"\\n```\\n\\n### Step 3: 方案落地\\n1. **仓库初始化**：git init → .gitignore → 首次提交\\n2. **分支创建**：git branch + git worktree add\\n3. **钩子配置**：写入幂等 post-checkout 钩子\\n4. **Agent绑定**：每个分支分配唯一Agent\\n5. **后端配置**：更新 OPENAI_BASE_URL 到实际模型后端\\n\\n### Step 4: 结果验证\\n- 标记文件生成验证\\n- 幂等跳过验证（二次运行应跳过）\\n- 端口监听验证\\n- API连通性测试（curl /v1/models）\\n- 会话检索验证（session_search）\\n\\n### Step 5: 迭代沉淀\\n- 归档报告到 Doc/ 目录\\n- 更新 memory 中的环境信息\\n- 如发现新坑，更新本技能文档\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 新增：会话持久化保障规则（V2.6）\\n\\n### 规则 1: state.db 是唯一真相源\\n- Hermes 的所有会话存储在 `state.db`（SQLite），实时落盘\\n- 会话数据不因 Orca 重启、Agent 重启、分支切换丢失\\n\\n### 规则 2: Orca UI 面板限制的应对\\n- `workspaceSessionsByHostId` 重启后清空 → UI 面板空白\\n- 应对：输入「查看历史会话」从 state.db 检索\\n- 备选：将会话导出为 Markdown 文件存入 Doc/\\n\\n### 规则 3: 检索指令标准化\\n```\\n查看历史会话                          → 最近10条会话\\n查找 <关键词> 的历史对话              → 关键词检索\\n查看 <日期> 的会话                    → 按日期筛选\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 新增：Codex 端口与后端适配规则（V2.6）\\n\\n### 规则 1: 不依赖 Orca 代理端口\\n- Codex 通过 stdio 与 Orca 通信，Orca 代理端口（如15721）为临时分配\\n- 配置中使用模型后端直连 URL，不使用 Orca 代理地址\\n\\n### 规则 2: OPENAI_BASE_URL 配置规范\\n| 后端 | URL | 说明 |\\n|------|-----|------|\\n| MIMO | `https://token-plan-cn.xiaomimimo.com/v1` | 首选，无需本地服务 |\\n| Ollama | `http://127.0.0.1:11434/v1` | 需确保 Ollama 运行 |\\n| OpenAI | `https://api.openai.com/v1` | 需 API Key |\\n\\n### 规则 3: API Key 管理\\n- Key 存储在 `.env`，配置文件引用\\n- 如 Key 被截断为占位符，从 `.env` 重新写入\\n- 禁止在日志/报告中输出完整 Key\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 快速参考\\n\\n| 操作 | 命令 |\\n|------|------|\\n| 检查Orca进程 | `tasklist \\\\| \"代理转发返回 404\"\r\n  - \"stream error upstream_error\"\r\n  - \"CC Switch URL 路径拼接异常\"\r\n  - \"Codex/Claude Code 无法通过代理连通模型\"\r\n---\r\n\r\n# CC Switch 代理链路排障技能 v1.0\r\n\r\n## 适用场景\r\n- CC Switch 代理转发请求到上游模型时返回 404 Not Found\r\n- Codex/Claude Code 出现 `stream error: upstream_error` 或 `upstream_status: 404`\r\n- URL 路径拼接异常（如缺少 `/v1` 前缀、端点路径被剥离）\r\n\r\n## 前置约束\r\n- **禁止绕过 CC Switch 直连模型**，所有修复必须在代理侧完成\r\n- 修改 CC Switch 数据库前必须先终止 `cc-switch.exe` 进程\r\n\r\n## 排障步骤\r\n\r\n### 第1步：诊断上游连通性\r\n```bash\r\n# 测试模型 API 直连（确认上游服务正常）\r\ncurl -s -w \"\\nHTTP %{http_code}\" \"https://token-plan-cn.xiaomimimo.com/v1/chat/completions\" \\\r\n  -H \"Authorization: Bearer *** \\\r\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\r\n  -d '{\"model\":\"mimo-v2.5-pro\",\"messages\":[{\"role\":\"user\",\"content\":\"hi\"}],\"max_tokens\":5}'\r\n# 预期: HTTP 200\r\n\r\n# 测试 CC Switch 代理转发\r\ncurl -s -w \"\\nHTTP %{http_code}\" \"http://127.0.0.1:15721/v1/chat/completions\" \\\r\n  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\\r\n  -d '{\"model\":\"mimo-v2.5-pro\",\"messages\":[{\"role\":\"user\",\"content\":\"hi\"}],\"max_tokens\":5}'\r\n```\r\n\r\n### 第2步：检查 CC Switch 日志定位根因\r\n```bash\r\ntail -20 \"/c/Users/Administrator/.cc-switch/logs/cc-switch.log\"\r\n# 关键字段:\r\n# \"请求 URL: https://...\" → 实际发送的上游 URL\r\n# \"endpoint: /chat/completions\" → CC Switch 识别的端点\r\n# \"upstream_status: 404\" → 上游返回状态码\r\n```\r\n\r\n### 第3步：修复 meta.isFullUrl 配置（根因修复点）\r\n```python\r\n\n===== 现有修复脚本 =====\ntotal 8\ndrwxr-xr-x 1 Administrator 197121    0 Jul  9 09:14 .\ndrwxr-xr-x 1 Administrator 197121    0 Jul  9 09:14 ..\n-rwxr-xr-x 1 Administrator 197121 5412 Jul  9 09:14 fix_cc_switch_routing.py", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

|\n|View all files |\n\n## Repository files navigation\n\n* README\n* MIT license\n\n🇺🇸 English | 🇰🇷 한국어\n\n* * *\n\n# Claude Code Agent Teams on Windows — psmux Split Panes\n> **World's first native Windows solution** for running Claude Code agent teams\n> in split terminal panes — without WSL, without Cygwin, without any Linux subsystem.\n> \n> \n\n[Windows](https://camo.githubusercontent.com/92babd45a6b5a47b540685fa4a4048e48ac7e671dff7fe5c521a338711565a1b/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f62616467652f57696e646f77732d31312d626c75653f6c6f676f3d77696e646f7773) [psmux](https://camo.githubusercontent.com/2a854b605ffb3b4c4b3ebe59892b0a68abe62e4b74c9448101382622ffa815c0/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f62616467652f70736d75782d76302e342e31302d6f72616e6765) [Claude Code](https://camo.githubusercontent.com/2a5017a24f13a5aa89be2a7ff7c87aac80bee0b4ee829f113101c541455e189a/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f62616467652f436c617564655f436f64652d76322e312e37312d626c756576696f6c6574) [License](https://camo.githubusercontent.\ncom/f8df3091bbe1149f398a5369b2c39e896766f9f6efba3477c63e9b4aa940ef14/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f62616467652f6c6963656e73652d4d49542d677265656e)\n\n## What This Does\n\nClaude Code's [agent team feature](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/agent-teams) lets you spawn multiple AI teammates that collaborate on tasks in parallel.\nIn split-pane mode, each teammate gets its own visible terminal pane — you can\nwatch all of them working simultaneously.\n\nThis is officially supported on macOS via **tmux** or **iTerm2** .\nOn **Windows** , it was previously only possible inside WSL (Ubuntu) —\nrequiring syncing files between Windows and the Linux subsystem.\n\n**This project makes it work natively on Windows** using [psmux](https://github.com/marlocarlo/psmux) , a Rust-based tmux clone\nthat runs without WSL.\n\n```\npsmux session (current behavior)\n┌──────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────┐\n│ Pane 1  Lead             │ Pane 2  Teammates (cycling)   │\n│ Claude Code v2.1.50      │ @alpha → completes            │\n│ Opus 4.6 · orchestrating │ @beta  → completes            │\n│                          │ @gamma → completes            │\n└──────────────────────────┴───────────────────────────────┘\n shift-tab to cycle between active teammates\n```\n\n> **Note** : Claude Code currently creates one shared pane for all teammates\n> (they run sequentially). Per-teammate panes (as on macOS) require a Claude\n> Code update. See docs/issues-and-improvements.md .\n> \n> \n\n## Prior Art\n\nThe only previously known Windows approach was WSL-based: [treylom/claude-agent-teams-setup](https://github.com/treylom/claude-agent-teams-setup) — runs tmux inside Ubuntu on WSL, then syncs the working directory back to Windows.\nThis project eliminates that overhead entirely.\n\n## Requirements\n\n...\n\n|`display-message` format vars |Shim required |Native |\n|`send-keys -t %N` (bare pane ID) |Shim required |Native |\n|`split-window -P -F #{pane_id}` |Shim required |Native |\n|`kill-pane -t %N` |Shim required |Native |\n|Per-teammate panes |Not supported |Not supported\\* |\n\n_\\*Claude Code creates 1 split for all teammates. This is a Claude Code behavior, not a psmux limitation._\n\n## Contributing\n\nIssues and PRs welcome! See docs/issues-and-improvements.md for the full list of known issues and suggest",

        "title": "[FEATURE] Agent Teams: add Windows Terminal split pane support · Issue #31297 · anthropics/claude-code · GitHub",
        "description": "## Navigation Menu\n\nToggle navigation\n\nAppearance settings\n\nSearch or jump to...\n\n# Search code, repositories, users, issues, pull requests...\n\nSearch\n\nClear\n\n[Search syntax tips](https://docs.github.com/search-github/github-code-search/understanding-github-code-search-syntax)\n\nAppearance settings\n\nResetting focus\n\nYou signed in with another tab or window. Reload to refresh your session. You signed out in another tab or window. Reload to refresh your session. You switched accounts on another tab or window. Reload to refresh your session. Dismiss alert\n\nanthropics / **claude-code** Public\n\n* Notifications You must be signed in to change notification settings\n* Fork 18\\.4k\n* Star 111k\n\n# [FEATURE] Agent Teams: add Windows Terminal split pane support \\#31297\n\nNew issue\n\nCopy link\n\nNew issue\n\nCopy link\n\nClosed\n\nClosed\n\n[[FEATURE] Agent Teams: add Windows Terminal split pane support]() \\#31297\n\nCopy link\n\nLabels\n\n[area:agents](https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues?\n\n...\n\nAgent Teams `display` modes currently support `tmux` (Linux/macOS) and `iTerm2` (macOS) for split pane viewing of teammate activity. On Windows, the only available option is `in-process` , which shows all teammates in a single pane with Shift+Down to cycle through them.\n\nThis makes monitoring 3+ teammates impractical — you can only see one at a time and must manually cycle. Windows is a significant portion of the developer market, and Windows users currently get a degraded Agent Teams experience.\n\n### Proposed Solution\n\nAdd a `display: wt` mode that uses Windows Terminal's native CLI for split panes:\n\n```\nwt -w 0 sp -H --title \" teammate-1 \" cmd /c \" claude ... \" \nwt -w 0 sp -V --title \" teammate-2 \" cmd /c \" claude ... \"\n```\n\nWindows Terminal (default on Windows 11) supports:\n\n* Horizontal ( `-H` ) and vertical ( `-V` ) splits via `wt` CLI\n* Named panes with `--title`\n* Programmable focus switching\n* The `wt` command is available system-wide\n\nDetection: check if `wt.\nexe` is available on PATH (present on all Windows 11 installs and Windows 10 with Windows Terminal installed).\n\n### Alternative Solutions\n\n1. **`display: web`** — a localhost web dashboard showing all teammate activity in a browser (cross-platform, would work everywhere)\n2. **`display: log`** — each teammate writes to a separate log file, user monitors with `tail -f` or similar\n3. **Current workaround:** using `in-process` mode and cycling through teammates with Shift+Down — functional but impractical for 3+ teammates\n\n### Priority\n\nMedium - Would be very helpful\n\n### Feature Category\n\nInteractive mode (TUI)\n\n### Use Case Example\n\n1. I have a conductor agent that spawns 3-4 teammates (laravel-dev, frontend-dev, security-auditor, code-reviewer)\n2. I want to monitor all teammates working simultaneously\n3. With `display: wt` , Windows Terminal would split into panes, each showing a teammate's progress in real time\n4.\nThis matches the experience that tmux/iTerm2 users already have on Linux/macOS\n\n### Additional Context\n\n* This is documented as a known limitation in the official docs\n* Windows Terminal `wt` CLI reference: <https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/terminal/command-line-arguments>\n* The `wt` approach is analogous to how `tmux` and `iTerm2` modes work — launching subprocesses in terminal-managed panes\n* Agent Teams is an experimental feature enabled via `CLAUDE_CODE_EXPERIMENTAL_AGENT_TEAMS=1`\n\nReactions are currently unavailable\n\n## Metadata\n\n## Metadata\n\n### Assignees\n\nNo one assigned\n\n### Labels\n\n[area:agents](https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues?q=state%3Aopen%20label%3A%22area%3Aagents%22) [area:tui](https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues?q=state%3Aopen%20label%3A%22area%3Atui%22) [enhancement New feature or request](https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues?\nq=state%3Aopen%20label%3A%22enhancement%22) New feature or request [platform:windows Issue specifically occurs on Windows](https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues?q=state%3Aopen%20label%3A%22platform%3Awindows%22) Issue specifically occurs on Windows\n\n### Type\n\nNo type\n\n### Fields\n\n[Give feedback](https://github.com/orgs/community/discussions/189141)\n\nNo fields configured for issues wit",

The project is maintained by Stably AI and has grown to nearly 12,000 GitHub stars with 800 forks as of mid-2026. The repository demonstrates active development with a median issue and pull request response latency of zero hours and a mean response time of 9.5 hours across over 1,200 tracked items, indicating a highly responsive development team.\nThe core functionality of Orca centers on running multiple coding agents simultaneously, each operating within isolated git worktrees. Users can execute agents like Codex, ClaudeCode, OpenCode, or Pi side-by-side and compare their outputs before merging the best results. This parallel execution model is designed to accelerate development workflows by allowing multiple approaches to be explored concurrently. The application supports running agents with users' own API subscriptions, giving developers control over their infrastructure and costs.\n\nThe platform includes a mobile companion application available on iOS through the App Store and TestFlight, as well as Android via APK distribution. The mobile app enables users to monitor and steer agents remotely, receive notifications when agents complete tasks, and send follow-up instructions from anywhere. This mobile-first approach extends the IDE experience beyond the desktop environment.\nOrca provides terminal functionality comparable to Ghostty-class terminals, featuring WebGL rendering, infinite splits, and persistent scrollback that survives application restarts. The integrated browser includes a Design Mode that allows users to click UI elements in a real Chromium window and automatically send the corresponding HTML, CSS, and cropped screenshots directly into agent prompts. Native integrations with GitHub and Linear enable users to browse pull requests, issues, and project boards within the application, opening worktrees from tasks without context switching.\n\nThe platform supports SSH worktrees for running agents on remote machines with full file editing, git operations, and terminal access, including automatic reconnection and port forwarding. Users can annotate AI-generated diffs with comments that are sent back to agents for review and editing before committing.\nThe application includes a VS Code-compatible editor with autosave functionality and supports dragging files and images directly into agent prompts.\n\nOrca CLI provides scriptable access to workflows through commands like worktree create, snapshot, click, and fill, enabling agents to drive Orca itself. Additional features include quick open search across worktrees, files, agents, and commands, account switching with usage tracking for Claude and Codex APIs, rich previews for Markdown and PDFs, and Computer Use capabilities allowing agents to interact with desktop applications.\n\nThe most active contributors tracked by GitGenius are AmethystLiang with 1,907 events, Jinwoo-H with 1,022 events, and nwparker with 784 events. Orca ships two keyboard-first navigation tools.\n\n## Quick Open (Cmd-P)\n\nFile search scoped to the current worktree. Type a fragment; Orca ranks by recency plus match score and opens the file in a new editor tab. Gitignored files are included in results — they're surfaced as a second pass after tracked matches, so the files you frequently quick-open (build outputs, env files) stay reachable without polluting the top of the list.\n\n## Worktree Jump Palette (Cmd-J)\n\nJump across every worktree and every tab in one search. The placeholder in the empty input reads _repo/worktree_ — type either half and Orca filters accordingly. Once you start typing, search includes non-archived worktrees even if they are hidden by the sidebar's current filters.\n\nResults include:\n\n* **Recent Worktrees** — the top section with an empty query, ordered by last focus, capped so the list stays scannable.\n* Projects and repo groups, so you can jump to a sidebar section by name.\n* Every worktree grouped by repo once you start typing.\n* Every open tab, scoped first by current worktree, then globally.\n\nShift-Enter on a worktree opens it in a new split instead of swapping the current pane.\n\nWhen the query does not match an existing worktree, the palette offers a **Create worktree** row using the typed text as the name.         "description": "Discovery\n\nBrowse Collections [Essays ↗](https://www.niravjoshi.dev/blog)\n\nHome / Browse\n\n# Orca\n\nStably's next-gen IDE that's built around running coding agents in parallel. First-class support for Claude Code, Codex, Cursor agent, OpenCode, Ghostty, and worktree-based orchestration.\n\n[repo ↗](https://github.com/stablyai/orca)\n\n\\#claude-code \\#codex \\#ide \\#orchestration \\#multi-agent\n\nMost “AI IDEs” are an editor with an LLM tab bolted on the side. Orca is the inverse: an IDE designed from the ground up around running coding agents in parallel, with the editor functions arranged to serve that workflow. Stably ships it for macOS, Windows, and Linux, and the install model is “bring your own subscription” - no Orca-side login, no Orca-side key.\n\nThe thing it gets right that side-tab IDEs don’t: every feature is a worktree. Spinning up a new agent task creates a new worktree automatically; switching between tasks is switching worktrees.\n\n...\n\n* **Multi-agent terminals** \\- run several agents side by side in tabs and panes; a status indicator shows which are active.\n* **Built-in source control** \\- review AI-generated diffs, make quick edits, commit, all without leaving Orca.\n* **GitHub integration** \\- PRs, issues, and Actions checks linked to each worktree automatically.\n* **SSH support** \\- connect to remote machines and run agents on them directly from the IDE.\n* **Notifications** \\- mark threads unread to come back to later.\n\nThe piece worth highlighting: **Annotate AI Diff** . You can comment directly on lines of an AI-generated diff and send the annotated review back to the agent for revision. No copying line numbers, no context-switching to a chat window. The review loop stays inside the diff viewer.\n\n## Hot-swap Codex accounts\n\nThis one is for the people running multiple Codex subscriptions to chase the best token deal. Orca lets you switch accounts in one click - no re-login, no editing config files.\nPick an account, keep building. If that workflow describes you, this is the kind of feature that pays back the install cost in a single afternoon.\n\n## Per-worktree browser and Design Mode\n\nEach worktree gets its own embedded browser. Preview your app as you build, then flip into Design Mode and click any UI element - it lands in the chat as context for the agent. The agent gets a stable selector or component reference; you skip the screenshot-and-explain cycle.\n\nThis is the right primitive for frontend work specifically. Most “AI in the browser” tools are bookmarklets or extensions; building it into the worktree means it’s scoped correctly when you have five tasks open at once.\n\n## When to reach for it\n\n* You’ve outgrown a single agent inside a single editor and the parallel-worktree dance has become its own task.\n* You manage multiple accounts (Codex especially) and the hot-swap is genuinely useful.\n* You do a lot of frontend work where the click-an-element-into-chat pattern saves real time.\n\n## When not to\n\n* You’re already deeply invested in another IDE (Neovim, Emacs, JetBrains) and won’t migrate. Orca is the IDE; it’s not a plugin.\n* You want a fully open-source stack. The IDE is closed-source; the CLI piece is on GitHub.\n* Headless / server workflows. Orca is a desktop application - the right tool for that job is something like Agent of Empires running over SSH.\n\n## What’s not in the README\n\nThere’s no documented self-hosted or air-gapped mode. The IDE is a download; the agent CLIs you bring are the ones that matter for data flow. If you have an enterprise setup that needs a gateway in front of all model traffic, that’s a separate problem (ThinkWatch is a clean fit) - Orca ",

## 诊断与修复报告

### 根因分析

| 层次 | 问题 | 状态 |
|------|------|------|
| **Claude Code 层** | Agent Teams 使用 tmux 分屏，Windows 不支持 tmux | 已修复 → 改用 `in-process` 模式 |
| **Orca 层** | `experimentalNativeChat=False`，原生聊天界面未启用 | 已修复 → 启用图形化聊天框 |
| **Orca 层** | `openAgentTabsInChatByDefault=False`，Agent 默认在终端打开 | 已修复 → 默认以聊天模式打开 |
| **协议层** | Claude Code → MIMO 需要 Anthropic→OpenAI 协议转换 | 已就绪 → 中间件运行中 |

### 已修改的配置

**Orca** (`%APPDATA%\orca\orca-data.json`):
```
experimentalNativeChat: False → True  （启用图形化聊天界面）
openAgentTabsInChatByDefault: False → True  （Agent默认以聊天框打开）
claudeAgentTeamsMode: off → in-process  （Windows兼容模式）
```

**Claude Code** (`~/.claude/settings.json`):
```
CLAUDE_CODE_EXPERIMENTAL_AGENT_TEAMS=1  （启用Agent Teams）
CLAUDE_AGENT_TEAMS_DISPLAY=in-process  （Windows兼容，不用tmux）
```

### 验证结果：全部 ✅

```
✅ experimentalNativeChat: True
✅ openAgentTabsInChatByDefault: True
✅ claudeAgentTeamsMode: in-process
✅ CLAUDE_CODE_EXPERIMENTAL_AGENT_TEAMS: 1
✅ CLAUDE_AGENT_TEAMS_DISPLAY: in-process
✅ ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL: http://127.0.0.1:15721
✅ 协议转换中间件: 端口15721监听中
✅ 开机自启: 计划任务已注册
```

---

### 你现在需要做的

1. **重启 Orca**（必须，配置修改需要重启生效）
2. 在 Orca 中打开任意工作树
3. 按 **Cmd-J**（或 Ctrl-J）打开 Jump Palette
4. 输入 `Claude Code` 或 `Hermes`，选择智能体
5. 此时应以**图形化聊天框**打开（输入框 + 对话历史），而非终端黑窗口

备份文件：`%APPDATA%\orca\orca-data.json.bak.*`，如需回滚可恢复。

{"success": true, "diff": "--- a/E:\\龙虾AI主控中心\\我的AI分身\\CODEX量化策略20260615\\嘉盛\\黄金黑马策略\\scripts\\anthropic_to_openai_proxy.py\n+++ b/E:\\龙虾AI主控中心\\我的AI分身\\CODEX量化策略20260615\\嘉盛\\黄金黑马策略\\scripts\\anthropic_to_openai_proxy.py\n@@ -403,6 +403,60 @@\n             log(f\"代理错误: {e}\", \"ERROR\")\n             self.send_error_response(502, \"proxy_error\", str(e))\n \n+    def handle_openai_passthrough(self, openai_req):\n+        \"\"\"直接透传 OpenAI Chat Completions 请求到 MIMO\"\"\"\n+        is_stream = openai_req.get(\"stream\", False)\n+        log(f\"OpenAI 透传: /v1/chat/completions | stream={is_stream}\")\n+\n+        try:\n+            if is_stream:\n+                # 流式透传\n+                url = MIMO_BASE_URL + MIMO_CHAT_ENDPOINT\n+                headers = {\"Content-Type\": \"application/json\", \"Authorization\": f\"Bearer {self.api_key}\"}\n+                data = json.dumps(openai_req).encode(\"utf-8\")\n+                req = urllib.request.Request(url, data=data, headers=headers, method=\"POST\")\n+                ctx = ssl.create_default_context()\n+                ctx.check_hostname = False\n+                ctx.verify_mode = ssl.CERT_NONE\n+                resp = urllib.request.urlopen(req, context=ctx, timeout=120)\n+\n+                self.send_response(200)\n+                self.send_header(\"Content-Type\", \"text/event-stream\")\n+                self.send_header(\"Cache-Control\", \"no-cache\")\n+                self.send_header(\"Connection\", \"keep-alive\")\n+                self.send_cors_headers()\n+                self.end_headers()\n+\n+                buffer = \"\"\n+                while True:\n+                    chunk = resp.read(1)\n+                    if not chunk:\n+                        break\n+                    buffer += chunk.decode(\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\")\n+                    while \"\\n\" in buffer:\n+                        line, buffer = buffer.split(\"\\n\", 1)\n+                        if line.strip():\n+                            self.wfile.write((line + \"\\n\").encode(\"utf-8\"))\n+                    self.wfile.flush()\n+            else:\n+                # 非流式透传\n+                resp = forward_to_mimo(openai_req, self.api_key, stream=False)\n+                resp_body = resp.read()\n+                self.send_response(200)\n+                self.send_header(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n+                self.send_cors_headers()\n+                self.end_headers()\n+                self.wfile.write(resp_body)\n+\n+            log(f\"OpenAI 透传完成\")\n+        except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:\n+            error_body = e.read().decode(\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\")\n+            log(f\"OpenAI 透传错误 [{e.code}]: {error_body}\", \"ERROR\")\n+            self.send_error_response(e.code, \"api_error\", f\"Backend returned {e.code}\")\n+        except Exception as e:\n+            log(f\"OpenAI 透传异常: {e}\", \"ERROR\")\n+            self.send_error_response(502, \"proxy_error\", str(e))\n+\n     def handle_responses_api(self, responses_req):\n         \"\"\"处理 OpenAI Responses API 请求（Codex 专用）→ 转换为 Chat Completions\"\"\"\n         # ## 三部分交付报告

### 第一部分：Codex 接口 404 报错根因 + 修复

**根因**：
- Codex 配置 `wire_api = "responses"`，发送请求到 `/v1/responses`（OpenAI Responses API 格式）
- 协议转换中间件原只支持 `/v1/messages`（Anthropic）和 `/health`，缺少 `/v1/responses` 端点
- 同时发现 `/v1/chat/completions` 透传方法 `handle_openai_passthrough` 未定义

**修复**：
| 端点 | 状态 | 说明 |
|------|------|------|
| `/v1/messages` | ✅ | Anthropic → OpenAI 转换（Claude Code） |
| `/v1/responses` | ✅ 新增 | Responses API → Chat Completions 转换（Codex） |
| `/v1/chat/completions` | ✅ 新增 | 直接透传 OpenAI 格式 |
| `/health` | ✅ | 健康检查 |

**验证结果**：全部 4 个端点返回 HTTP 200 ✅

---

### 第二部分：Claude Code / Hermes 图形聊天框验收

**Orca 配置**（已修改，需重启 Orca 生效）：
```
experimentalNativeChat: True      ← 启用图形化聊天界面
openAgentTabsInChatByDefault: True ← Agent 默认以聊天框打开
claudeAgentTeamsMode: in-process   ← Windows 兼容模式
```

**Claude Code 配置**（已就绪）：
```
CLAUDE_CODE_EXPERIMENTAL_AGENT_TEAMS=1
CLAUDE_AGENT_TEAMS_DISPLAY=in-process
ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:15721
```

**验收步骤**：
1. **关闭 Orca** → **重新打开 Orca**
2. 打开任意工作树
3. 从 Agent 下拉菜单选择 **Claude Code** 或 **Hermes**
4. 应以图形化聊天框打开（输入框 + 对话历史），而非终端黑窗口
5. 如配置被回滚，运行一键脚本：`python scripts/setup_orca_native_chat.py`

---

### 第三部分：Codex 图形化聊天配置

Codex 已自动享受与 Claude Code / Hermes 相同的配置：
- `experimentalNativeChat: True` — 全局生效，覆盖所有 Agent
- `openAgentTabsInChatByDefault: True` — Codex 也以聊天框打开
- Codex `wire_api = "responses"` + 中间件 `/v1/responses` 端点 — 链路已通

**一键配置脚本**：`scripts/setup_orca_native_chat.py`
- 自动检测中间件状态并启动
- 自动修改 Orca / Claude Code 配置
- 自动执行全链路验证
- 输出配置结果汇总

---

```
